## 2026-05-29 — d24-p2-divisio CREATED (Tier-2) — opens d.24 Pars II

Created NEW `bon-sent-II-d24-p2-divisio` (auto-chunker dropped it). **COMMENTARIUS IN DISTINCTIONEM XXIV Pars II** (*De ordine et progressu peccati per vires animae*) + **DIVISIO TEXTUS** + **TRACTATIO QUAESTIONUM**, single printed **p. 573** (PDF 595, +22), raw 40134–40173. Latin re-set from 450 dpi PDF bands (`/tmp/colcrop/vol2-p573-{L,R}-{0,1,2}.png`). `has_scholion: false`.

- **SCOPE DECISION: ART I NOT folded.** The prior resume guess ("ART I = 1 q, fold per Override §5") was WRONG. Raw + PDF confirm **ART I has TWO full quaestiones** (q1 raw 40172 *…peccatum secundum se*; q2 raw 40346 *…possit esse veniale peccatum*, CONCLUSIO raw 40395), each with full videtur/contra/CONCLUSIO/Respondeo. Override §5 (fold only a *short* opener) does not apply. This chunk holds COMMENTARIUS + DIVISIO TEXTUS + TRACTATIO QUAESTIONUM only. ## (archived) 2026-05-29 — d21-a3-q3 promoted

Promoted `bon-sent-II-d21-a3-q3` Tier-2 (*Utrum primum peccatum fuerit gravissimum omnium peccatorum.*):

- QUAESTIO III (**the LAST quaestio of ARTICULUS III**, *De quantitate peccati primorum parentum*). Spans **pp. 508–509** (PDF 530–531, +22). Opener p.508 L (raw 35674): *Tertio quaeritur, utrum peccatum, quod ex illa tentatione subsecutum est, fuerit gravissimum omnium peccatorum. Et quod sic, videtur.* Arg pro 1–4 (Augustin. XIV de Civ. Dei *Magna fuit iniquitas ubi tanta facilitas* + maior facilitas obediendi→maior culpa contemnendi; Augustin. ibidem *obediens usque ad mortem* Phil 2,8 → max meritum/max culpa inobedientis usque ad mortem; peccatum corruptio modi speciei ordinis → maxime corruptivum totum genus; punitum poena mortis+exhaeredationis = maxima poena). *Sed contra* 1–4 (remissibile<irremissibile + supra probatum remissibile; gravius quod nullo modo excusabile, sed Adae habuit excusationem ex aliena instigatione, peccatum in Spiritum sanctum colorem excusationis non habet; gravius ex maiori libidine, sed in primis parentibus natura munda+sana minime regnavit libido; gravius contra rationem+naturam+institutionem quam solum contra mandatum, sed Adae solum contra divinam institutionem). CONCLUSIO (*Peccatum Adae non fuit gravissimum propter maiorem libidinem vel ingratitudinem, fuit tamen aliqualiter sub duplici respectu gravissimum omnium humanorum peccatorum*). Respondeo p.508 R→509: tres modi gravitatis — (1) libido: nec maximum simpliciter nec humanorum (diabolus + filii Adae ex maiori libidine); (2) ingratitudo: non maximum simpliciter (diabolus plura bona+sublimior status) sed maximum omnium humanorum (Adam nobiliori statu); (3) laesio: intensive non maximae laesionis (non adeo depravavit voluntatem), extensive maximae (tot personis nocuit, ex tentatione primaria). Epilogus: *Et ex hoc patet responsio ad rationes ad utramque partem adductas* (utraeque secundum diversas vias verum concludunt). SCHOLION I+II p.509.
- **9 apparatus entries [^1]–[^9]** renumbered continuously. Page-split map:
  - p.508 footers ¹–⁸ = [^1]–[^8]: Aug. de Civ.Dei XIV c.15 n.1; Aug. loc.cit. textus integer (Phil 2,8); Aug. de Natura boni c.4+37 + d.33 a.2 + contra Iulianum + Enchir. + Magister d.XXXIII c.3; codd. F ee *exhaereditationis*; Quaest. praeced. + Matth 12,31 (Spirit-blasphemy) + codd. *maior* variant; Cfr. infra d.43 a.2 q.1; codd. *dicebat* for *dicatur*; Vat. added *humanorum*.
  - p.509 footer ¹ = [^9] **received into q3's conclusio** (anchors *intelligi et esse*): codd. cc/ed.1 omit *et* + Cfr. infra d.33 dub.2 (twelve modes one sin graver than another). The content ("unum peccatum gravius esse altero") proves it belongs to q3's conclusio, not to DUB I (about *locutio per serpentem*).
- `has_scholion: true`. SCHOLION I = S.Thom. distinction (gravity by *species* essential vs by *condition of those sinning*) + Scotus dissent (Adam minorem poenam) + Richard. a Med. consent. SCHOLION II = commentator-roll (Alex.Hal. q.104 m.3, B.Albert tr.14 q.87 m.3, Petr. a Tar. q.3 a.2, Aegid.R. a.2 q.2, Durand. q.3, Biel q.2).

## (archived) 2026-05-29 — d21-a3-q2 promoted

Promoted `bon-sent-II-d21-a3-q2` Tier-2 (*Utrum peccatum primum hominis fuerit remissibile, vel irremissibile.*):

- QUAESTIO II of **ARTICULUS III** (*De quantitate peccati primorum parentum*). Spans **pp. 506–507** (PDF 528–529, +22). Opener p.506 L (raw 35540): *Secundo quaeritur, utrum peccatum, quod primo secutum est ex tentatione illa, fuerit remissibile, vel irremissibile. Et quod irremissibile, videtur.* *Ad oppositum* args 1–4 (Augustin. de Mirabilibus sacrae Scripturae on the Angel fell from sublimissimo statu; peccatum sine inclinatione→sine excusatione→irremissibile; morbus totam naturam inficit incurabilis; Adam habuit naturalia+gratuita Glossa Lucae 10 *vulneratus in naturalibus exspoliatus gratuitis* vs angelus solum naturalia). *Sed contra* args 1–4 (poenitentia via ad veniam, Adam doluit+poenituit; qui ab alio deiicitur ab alio adiuvetur; si irremissibile→tota natura damnata→providentia frustrata; culpa non maioris stabilitatis quam gratia). CONCLUSIO (*Peccatum primum hominis remissibile fuit, triplici ratione*). Respondeo p.506 R→507 L: three rationes — Ratio I *carnis mortalitas* (divine justice exercised via death+Christ's satisfaction, angel immortal nisi morte aeterna); Ratio II *voluntatis vertibilitas* (homo viator longo tempore, potuit transgredi→potuit conteri; angel lacked per brevitatem spatii merendi supra d.7); Ratio III *excusabilitas in culpa* (alio suggerente cecidit→mediatorem habere; Magister in littera). Epilogus: praecipua = Ratio II (vertibilitas voluntatis). Solutio Ad 1–4 p.507 R. SCHOLION I+II p.507 bottom.
- **15 apparatus entries [^1]–[^15]** renumbered continuously. Page-split map:
  - p.506 footers ¹–⁸ = [^1]–[^8]: August. de Mirab. Libr.I c.2 (Vide supra p.176 n.5); Cfr. infra d.30 a.1 q.1; Dist.29 a.2 q.1 + long Glossa-super-Luc.10/Beda/Ambros./August. provenance note; Dist.4 a.1 q.2; codd. variants for *inveniendam veniam* + Cfr. infra lit. proceeds without `d15-p1-*` or `d15-p2-*` files; see chunk list below.

## Convention reminders

- "keep going" = continue chunk-by-chunk, committing each, no check-ins
  (cf. [[feedback_bonaventure-bucket-e-autopilot]]).
- Tier-2 = literal, not paraphrase, incl. scholia/apparatus.
- Marginal labels (*Ad oppositum.*, *Fundamenta.*, *Ratio …*,
  *Solutio …*, *Notandum.*, *Alia solutio.*, *Dubium N.*, etc.) are
  preserved inline per Vol II convention, NOT promoted to headings.
- Backup before any rebuild: `cp <chunk>.md _backup-<chunk>-pre-<reason>-<YYYYMMDD>/`.
- Vol II offset: `pdf = printed + 22`. Trust running-head text, never
  the OCR'd digits.

Prior session-by-session vol2 history (sessions 1–77) is trimmed per
Wilson's request 2026-05-22; the per-decade resolution logs and chunk
`## Notes` are the durable record. Backup of the pre-trim resume is at
`_backup-resume-pre-trim-20260522.md` if you need to walk back.
